Files: `scripts/build-modules.mjs` (new, < 500 lines), `scripts/lib/module-db.mjs` (new), `scripts/modules-stage.mjs` (new), `content/library/modules.json` (new, tracked, written only by `build-modules.mjs`), `package.json` (`scripts` only), `vite.config.ts` (workbox `globIgnores` only), `.gitignore`, `.gitattributes`, the `'android-release'` stage command body (one line; `scripts/release-state.mjs` `stageCommands`, or `scripts/release-stage-commands.mjs` if BA.9 has already moved it there).
  - **Do:**
    - **Consumes (exact; code against these, never edit them):**
      - BA.10's finalized full corpus `build/corpus/missal.<V>.full.db` (or `<dir>/missal.<V>.full.db`). It contains `nodes`, `edges`, `text_blocks`, `embeddings` (dim 256), `search`, `corpus_meta`, `embed_meta` (exactly 7 rows: `model, artifact_sha256, dim, quant, prefix_query, prefix_doc, norm`), `embed_progress(phase, completed_at)` with a `finalize` row, `edges_trigram_archive(src_key,dst_key,rel,weight,meta)` and `embeddings_trigram`. `<V>` = `corpus_meta.corpus_version`.
      - BA.11's Catena graph inside that file:
        - `commentary` nodes `commentary:catena-aurea/<Book>/<ch>/<verseStart>`, with meta `ord`, `chapterFile` and `moduleId`;
        - `quotation` nodes `catena:<Book>/<ch>/<verseStart>/q<n>`, with meta `citesUnresolved:int`;
        - `father` nodes `father:<slug>`.
      - BA.9 (release partial §2.6): `scripts/release-inputs.mjs --vendor <dir>` vendors this task's outputs into `VENDORED/`. The corpus pointer is `VENDORED/corpus/CURRENT.json` `{schema:'sanctissimissa-corpus-pointer.v1', corpus_version, file, sha256, size_bytes, previous}`.
      - This task writes **no** pointer, resolver, `corpus:restore`, `sync-db.mjs`/`build.rs` edit or `modules-deploy.mjs`. Those are BA.9's (see Notes).
    - **`scripts/lib/module-db.mjs`** exports:
      - `MODULE_DDL`: the §2.3 DDL verbatim (`module_meta`, `embed_meta`, `mnodes` with `ord INTEGER NOT NULL`, `mtext`, `medges` with PK `(src_key,dst_key,rel)`, `memb`, and `CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE msearch USING fts5(key, section, content)` with no `tokenize=`);
      - `MODULE_KEY_RE = /^(commentary:catena-aurea\/|catena:|father:)/`;
      - `MODULE_EDGE_RELS = ['PART_OF','ATTRIBUTED_TO','CITES','COMMENTS_ON','INSTANCE_OF','SIMILAR_TO']`;
      - `EMBED_META_KEYS` (the 7 keys);
      - `contentDigest(db): string`. It is sha256 over the canonical dump: tables in order `embed_meta, mnodes, mtext, medges, memb, msearch`; rows `ORDER BY` primary key (msearch by `key`); fields tab-joined; BLOBs lowercase hex; NULL as the empty string; rows joined with `\n`; `module_meta` excluded.
    - **`scripts/build-modules.mjs`**, CLI `node --experimental-strip-types scripts/build-modules.mjs <full.db> [--out <dir>]`.
      - Defaults: base output → `assets/corpus/`; module output → `build/modules/`. With `--out <dir>`, both go to `<dir>`.
      - Exit codes (Closed set, Phase L): 0 ok/unchanged, 3 output exists, 4 invalid input.
      - It opens the input read-only. It refuses with exit 4 (`input lacks complete embed_meta / embed_progress.finalize`) unless all 7 `embed_meta` rows and the `finalize` row are present.
      - **(a) Module.** Create `<moduleOut>/.catena-aurea-en.tmp.db` with `MODULE_DDL`, then fill it:
        - `embed_meta`: copied from the input.
        - `mnodes`: every node whose key matches `MODULE_KEY_RE`, inserted in this order:
          - Book TOC order `Matt, Marc, Luc, Joann`, then `meta.chapterFile` ascending, then pericope `meta.ord` ascending;
          - each pericope is followed by its quotations, by ordinal;
          - then the 51 father nodes, by `scripts/catena-fathers.mjs` table order if that file exists, otherwise by key.
          - `ord` is the 1-based insertion counter. Columns are copied from `nodes`.
        - `mtext`: from `text_blocks`.
        - `memb`: from `embeddings` where `dim=256`.
        - `msearch`: the `search` rows whose `key` is in the set.
        - `medges`: every edge whose src **or** dst is in the set, with `rel` in `MODULE_EDGE_RELS`, written as `(src_key, dst_key, rel, weight, meta)`. `INSERT OR IGNORE` handles PK duplicates.
      - Compute `contentDigest`. If `content/library/modules.json` has a `catena-aurea-en` entry whose `contentDigest` is equal, delete the tmp file, print `unchanged` and exit 0.
      - Otherwise `N = previous revision + 1`, or 1 when there is no entry. If `<moduleOut>/catena-aurea-en-r<N>.db` exists, exit 3.
      - Write the `module_meta` rows (Closed set, Phase L):
        - `schema_version` `1`; `module_id` and `edition_id` both `catena-aurea-en`; `revision` `<N>`; `title` `Catena Aurea`; `corpus_version` `<V>`; `built_at` (ISO).
        - `content_digest`.
        - `source_sha256`: sha256 of the 89 files `VENDORED/catena-aurea/catena-aurea-project/markdown_chapters/*/*.md`, concatenated in path-sorted order.
        - `nucleus_source` `catena-aurea`; `authority_kind` `patristics`.
        - `nucleus_manifest`: JSON of §2.9 verbatim, with `sha256` = `source_sha256`.
        - `node_count`, `edge_count`, `quotation_count`, `father_count`.
        - `cites_unresolved`: `SUM(json_extract(meta,'$.citesUnresolved'))` over quotation rows, or `0`.
      - Rename tmp → `catena-aurea-en-r<N>.db`.
      - **(b) Base.** `<baseOut>/missal.<V>.db` must not exist (else exit 3). Run `VACUUM INTO` it from the input. Then, in the **copy** only, in one transaction:
        - for module-set node ids, delete `search` rows (by key), `embeddings`, `text_blocks`, and `edges` with src or dst in the set;
        - delete `edges_trigram_archive` rows whose `src_key` or `dst_key` matches;
        - delete the `nodes`;
        - `INSERT OR REPLACE corpus_meta('modules_split','catena-aurea-en')`.
        - Then `VACUUM`.
        - Postcondition, else exit 4: `SELECT count(*) FROM nodes WHERE key LIKE 'commentary:catena-aurea/%' OR key LIKE 'catena:%' OR key LIKE 'father:%'` = 0. The input file is never written.
      - **(c) `content/library/modules.json`.** Schema §2.4 exactly, 2-space JSON plus a trailing newline:
        - `{schema:1, generatedAt, modules:[{moduleId:'catena-aurea-en', editionId:'catena-aurea-en', title:'Catena Aurea', file:'catena-aurea-en-r<N>.db', revision:N, appVersion:<version.json version>, corpusVersion:'<V>', sizeBytes, sha256, contentDigest, entitlementId:'reference_catena-aurea-en', nucleusSource:'catena-aurea', authorityKind:'patristics', androidPack:'module_catena_aurea_en', embedMeta:{7 keys from embed_meta}}]}`.
      - **(d)** Print one line for BA.9's vendoring: `vendor-input base=<path> sha256=<hex> corpusVersion=<V> module=<path> revision=<N>`.
    - **`scripts/modules-stage.mjs`**, CLI `node scripts/modules-stage.mjs [--android-pack]`. It reads `content/library/modules.json` (absent → print `no modules.json — nothing to stage` and exit 0).
      - For each entry, the source is the first existing of:
        1. `VENDORED/modules/<file>`;
        2. `VENDORED/modules/sanctissimissa-module-<editionId>-r<revision>.db`;
        3. `build/modules/<file>`.
      - The source's sha256 must equal `entry.sha256`, else exit 3. No source → print `module <file> not present — skipped` and continue (exit 0).
      - **Without the flag:**
        - Copy to `dist/sanctissimissa-v<APP>-module-<editionId>-r<revision>.db` (`<APP>` = `version.json` `version`) with `COPYFILE_EXCL`. Existing equal bytes → reuse; differing bytes → exit 3.
        - If `VENDORED/corpus/CURRENT.json` exists, stage `VENDORED/corpus/<file>` to `dist/sanctissimissa-v<APP>-corpus-<corpus_version>.db` under the same rule.
        - Write `dist/sanctissimissa-v<APP>-content-manifest.json` `{corpus:{file,corpusVersion,sha256,sizeBytes}|null, modules:[{editionId,revision,file,sha256,sizeBytes}]}` under the same differing-bytes rule.
      - **With `--android-pack`:** only copy each module to `src-tauri/gen/android/<androidPack>/src/main/assets/modules/<file>`, skipping it if identical. Any other `*.db` already in that directory is moved, never deleted, to `~/outbox/sanctissimissa/android-pack-stale/`.
    - **`package.json` scripts:**
      - `"corpus:modules": "node --experimental-strip-types scripts/build-modules.mjs"` (input path passed after `--`);

      - Exports `type ModuleEntry` (§2.4 entry), `interface InstalledModule { editionId; revision; sha256; sizeBytes; storedAt }`, `class ModuleInstaller` and the app singleton `export const moduleInstaller = new ModuleInstaller()`.
      - **`catalogue(): Promise<ModuleEntry[]>`:**
        - The floor is the bundled `content/library/modules.json`, read via `import.meta.glob('../../../content/library/modules.json', { eager: true, import: 'default' })`; an absent file gives an empty floor, never a build error.
        - It then fetches `${MODULE_BASE_URL}/modules.json` with `{cache:'no-store'}`. A remote entry replaces a floor entry only when its integer `revision` is strictly greater. A network failure leaves the floor.
      - **`install(editionId, signal: AbortSignal, onProgress: (f: number) => void): Promise<InstalledModule>`:**
        - It looks up the entry; an unknown id throws `Error('unknown-module')`.
        - **Bytes:** inside Android Tauri (`companionInvoke()` from `src/core/chat/runtime.ts` is defined and `/Android/.test(navigator.userAgent)`), first `invoke('module_pack_fetch', {pack: entry.androidPack, file: entry.file})` → `ArrayBuffer`. Any rejection falls back to the network.
        - **Network:** `fetch(`${MODULE_BASE_URL}/${entry.file}`, {signal})`, streamed, with progress from `Content-Length` or `entry.sizeBytes`.
        - **Check:** `crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256')` hex must equal `entry.sha256`, else throw `new AttachRefusedError('sha256-mismatch')`.
        - **Store:** IndexedDB DB `sanctissimissa.library.packages.v1` (version 1), store `packages` (keyPath `editionId`), value `{editionId, revision, sha256, sizeBytes, storedAt, bytes}`.
      - `load(editionId): Promise<Uint8Array|null>`, `remove(editionId): Promise<void>`, `installed(editionId): Promise<InstalledModule|null>` (consumed by BA.6's `#download-manager` "Update available" = catalogue revision > installed revision).
    - **`src/core/data/moduleAttach.ts`**, `attachModuleRows(base: Database, id: string, bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<AttachResult>`:
      - It opens `bytes` with `initSqlJs({ locateFile: () => wasmUrl })` (same import as `corpusDb.ts:8`).
      - It ensures `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS module_attach(module_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, revision INTEGER NOT NULL, attached_at TEXT NOT NULL, first_node_id INTEGER, last_node_id INTEGER, nucleus_source TEXT)`.
      - **Refusals**, each throwing `AttachRefusedError`:
        - `module_meta.schema_version ≠ '1'` → `schema-unsupported`.
        - The base `embed_meta` is missing, or any of the 7 keys `model, artifact_sha256, dim, quant, prefix_query, prefix_doc, norm` is absent or unequal between base and module → `embed-meta-incompatible`.
        - `id` is already in `module_attach` → `already-attached`.
      - **One transaction:**
        1. For `mnodes ORDER BY ord`: an existing `nodes.key` is kept, counted in `skippedExistingKeys`, and its id mapped. Otherwise `INSERT INTO nodes(kind,key,title,category,rank_class,rank_num,color,meta)`.
        2. For newly inserted nodes only, copy `mtext`→`text_blocks`, `memb`→`embeddings` and `msearch`→`search`.
        3. `medges` are resolved key→id through the map, else `SELECT id FROM nodes WHERE key=?`. Unresolvable edges are counted in `unresolvedEdges`; the rest go into `edges(src,dst,rel,weight,meta)`.
        4. Record the `module_attach` row (revision and `nucleus_source` from `module_meta`; first and last new node id).
      - Any error → `ROLLBACK` and rethrow. The module Database is always closed.

JSON in setting `entitlements.cache.v1` = `{schemaVersion:1, users:{<uid>:{verifiedAt, grants:[{entitlementId, grantedAt, expiresAt, verifiedAt, source}]}}}`. Rules: (1) `writeVerified` replaces that uid's grant list with the response's `isActive` grants (absent or inactive → removed, that uid only — the only revocation path) then `persist()`; (2) callers never call it on any failure, so failures never change the cache; (3) honouring is `computeAccess`'s rule; (4) other uids' entries are never touched; (5) unparseable JSON is copied verbatim to setting `entitlements.cache.v1.corrupt.<ISO now>` and an empty `{schemaVersion:1, users:{}}` starts.
  - **Do — `index.ts` (`EntitlementController`, the single authority):** `constructor({adapter: CommerceAdapter|null, cache: EntitlementCache, editions: EditionIndex, territory: () => string})`; `setUser(uid: string|null): Promise<void>` — non-null: `adapter.configure(uid)` (skip when adapter null) then `refresh()`; null: no SDK call, live grants and offers cleared, cache untouched, listeners notified; `refresh(): Promise<'verified'|'cached'|'signed-out'|'unconfigured'>` — adapter null → `'unconfigured'` (cache still honoured for the current uid); uid null → `'signed-out'`; else `Promise.all([adapter.customerInfo(), adapter.packages(OFFERING_BOOKSTORE)])`: success → `cache.writeVerified(uid, grants, adapter.channel)`, live grants set, package list kept, `source:'live'` → `'verified'`; failure → previous live state kept if any, else `cache.read(uid)` with `source:'cache'`, packages stay unloaded (`offersFor` → `null`) → `'cached'`; listeners notified once per refresh; `has(entitlementId): boolean` (honoured); `accessFor(editionId): AccessState` — calls `computeAccess` with `edition = editions.get(editionId)`, `territory()`, `Date.now()`, memoized per `(version, editionId)` so the same object identity is returned until state changes (required for `useSyncExternalStore`); `offersFor(editionId): PurchaseOffer[]|null` = `null` until packages load, else `offersFromPackages(editionId, packages, edition !== null && canPublishEdition(edition, territory()))`; `subscribe(l: () => void): () => void`; readonly `signedIn` (uid ≠ null), `configured` (adapter ≠ null), `version` (increments on every change). Emit `debugEvent('commerce', 'configure', {channel})` once per distinct uid and `debugEvent('commerce', 'refresh', {result})` per refresh (never keys, never uids' grants).

Default chapter constant `DEFAULT_CHAPTER = 'Matt/1'`.
    2. `editionSections.ts`: `editionSections(db: CorpusDb, editionId: string, chapterKey: string): ReaderSection[]`. For any editionId other than `catena-aurea-en`, or an unparseable chapterKey, return `[]`. Rows come from `db.sqlRows("SELECT n.key, n.title, n.meta, tb.english FROM nodes n JOIN text_blocks tb ON tb.node_id = n.id WHERE n.kind = 'commentary' AND n.key LIKE ?", ['commentary:catena-aurea/<Book>/%'])`. Keep rows whose `meta.chapterFile` equals the chapter number; when `chapterFile` is absent, use the key's chapter. Sort by `meta.ord` ascending, with ties broken by verseStart. One `ReaderSection` per block: `anchor` = `book:catena-aurea-en#<Book>/<keyCh>/<verseStart>`; `nodeKey` = the pericope key `commentary:catena-aurea/<Book>/<keyCh>/<verseStart>`; `title` = `chapterLabel(chapterKey)` on block 0 and `<keyCh>:<verseStart>` on later blocks; `meta` = `Catena Aurea`; `latin` = `null`; `english` = `tb.english`. The same file also exports `editionChapters(db, editionId): string[]`, the distinct chapterKeys present in the attached rows, ordered Matt, Marc, Luc, Joann and then by chapter ascending (89 when the module is attached; `[]` when not). It also exports `blockVerse(db, nodeKey): string|null`, the lowest-id `verse:` node reached by a `COMMENTS_ON` edge from that pericope (`SELECT v.key FROM edges e JOIN nodes c ON c.id = e.src JOIN nodes v ON v.id = e.dst WHERE e.rel = 'COMMENTS_ON' AND c.key = ? ORDER BY v.id LIMIT 1`). A failed query (module not attached, or a pre-BA.14 tree) returns `[]`/`null`, never throws.
    3. `bookmarks.ts` (§2 bookstore-ui): `toggleBookmark(sidecar: SidecarDb, editionId: string, nodeKey: string, chapterKey: string): Promise<boolean>` returns the new state. `readBookmarks(sidecar, editionId): BookmarkItem[]`, where `BookmarkItem = {nodeKey: string; chapterKey: string; createdAt: string}`. Setting `library.bookmarks.<editionId>` holds exactly `{"schemaVersion":1,"items":[…]}`. Toggle adds the item (`createdAt` = ISO now) or removes it. Items are kept in `createdAt` ascending order. Write with `setSetting`, then `await persist()`. If the JSON is malformed or `schemaVersion` is not 1, copy the raw string to `library.bookmarks.<editionId>.recovered` and restart with an empty list. Nothing is silently dropped.
    4. `progress.ts` (commerce §2.4): `readProgress(sidecar, editionId): {chapterKey: string; updatedAt: string}|null` (a malformed value or an unparseable chapterKey reads as null). `writeProgress(sidecar, editionId, chapterKey): void` writes `{"schemaVersion":1,"chapterKey":…,"updatedAt":<ISO>}` and persists, debounced 1 s per edition. `latestProgress(sidecar, editionIds: string[]): {editionId, chapterKey, updatedAt}|null` returns the greatest `updatedAt`. The Read action opens `readProgress(…)?.chapterKey ?? DEFAULT_CHAPTER`.

These 10 fields only, using type-only imports.

Unmounted (§4.3): `#main-nav-rail`, `#mobile-toggle`, `#import-book-btn`, `.filter-chips`, “Local SQLite corpus index: Optimal”, “Last opened today at Sext”, `.footer-bar`, `.specimen-flag`, the Concordance Verification block, “Export Citation (BibTeX)”, `#design-specimen-notice`. `#main-content` renders as `.main-column` without the id. Works come from `validateCatalogue(<static import of content/library/catalogue.seed.json>).works`. The readable set is works with `editionId` non-null and `entitlements.accessFor(editionId).state` in {free, owned, subscription}. Re-render on entitlement change through `useSyncExternalStore(entitlements.subscribe, () => entitlements.version)`. Bindings:
       - `#library-count-badge` reads `${visibleCards} Active Works`.

It shows the edition title, `chapterLabel`, and a progress fill = (1-based index of chapterKey in `editionChapters`) / `editionChapters.length`. `Resume Reading →` opens the reader.
       - Then `<AudiobookDock controller={audiobook()} />`, directly before `#download-manager` (audiobook §4).
       - `#download-manager` `.stat-pill`s: `${k} Volumes ready offline (${MB} MB)`, where k = editions whose `installer.load(id)` is non-null and MB = Σ byteLength / 1048576 to 1 decimal. `${u} Update available` appears iff u > 0, where u counts `catalogue()` entries whose `revision` is greater than the `module_attach.revision` for that module (`db.sqlRows('SELECT module_id, revision FROM module_attach', [])`; a query error means 0). `Check Updates` re-reads `catalogue()`. `Manage Storage` toggles a per-card `Remove download` button that calls `installer.remove(id)`. That removes package bytes only; annotations, bookmarks and progress are kept.

Then `.book-title` and `.book-author`. `.book-meta-row` has two spans: `English` and the edition line (`catena-aurea-en` → `Oxford 1841–1845, ed. J. H. Newman`; `haydock-1883-en` → `New York: Dunigan, 1883`). `.book-description` is the synopsis (Catena: the editionCopy synopsis string; Haydock: omitted). `.book-badges-row` shows `📝 N Notes`, where N counts `sidecar.list()` accompaniments with any anchor starting `book:<editionId>#`, and `🔖 N Bookmarks` = `readBookmarks(…).length`. A zero count omits its badge. `.storage-status` reads `Downloaded (X MB)` / `Not downloaded` (`.status-dot.cloud`) / `Included in the app` (Haydock). `.card-actions` has `Inspect` plus either `Open` (installed or free) or `Download` (entitled, not installed). `Download` calls `installer.install(id, ctrl.signal, f => …)` with label `Downloading… <round(f*100)>%` (disabled), then `load(id)` and `db.attachModule(id, bytes)`. A rejection logs `debugEvent('library', 'download.failed', {editionId, message}, 'warn')` and restores the `Download` label. Catena `Open` opens the reader at its progress or `Matt/1`. Haydock `Open` calls `onOpenKey('verse:Matt/1/1')`.

Haydock never shows these. Re-render through `useSyncExternalStore(audiobook().subscribe, audiobook().getState)`.

Not mounted: `⚡ … Graph Clusters Indexed`, `Refresh Graph`, `Insert in Homily`, `View Linkage`. Every specimen metric is omitted.

Pump triggers: open, play, pause, seek, chunk stored, chunk failed, voice ready, network change. Steps: voice not `ready` → return; `N = calibration.synthWorkers`; `rtf` = EWMA (α 0.3) of `wallSec / durationSec`; `rtfEff = rtf / N`; idle-fill iff ≥ 3 samples and `rtfEff ≤ 0.5`, else buffer. Priority 1: missing sentences from the playhead to chapter end. Buffer: `aheadSec` over the contiguous stored/inflight run from the playhead (stored → `durationSec`, inflight → `text.length × secPerChar`) minus `offsetSec`; while `inflight < N && aheadSec < 120` dispatch the next missing of that run, continuing into the next chapter's sentence 0 when the chapter ends inside the window. Idle-fill: while `inflight < N` dispatch Priority 1, then current chapter 0→playhead, then following chapters in order, stopping before a chapter whose admission (estimated bytes = Σ estimateSec × measured bytes/sec) would exceed the budget after eviction (keep current and next chapter). Dispatch: `(await voiceRuntime.getVoice()).synthesize(text, {signal, priority: 'background'})`, one `AbortController` per job.        
        ```ts
        export const EMBED_META_KEYS = ['model','artifact_sha256','dim','quant','prefix_query','prefix_doc','norm'] as const;
        export type EmbedMetaKey = (typeof EMBED_META_KEYS)[number];
        export type EmbedMeta = Readonly<Record<EmbedMetaKey, string>>;
        export const RUNTIME_EMBED_META: EmbedMeta = { model: 'qwen3-embedding:0.6b', artifact_sha256: '06507c7b42688469c4e7298b0a1e16deff06caf291cf0a5b278c308249c3e439', dim: '256', quant: 'int8', prefix_query: 'Instruct: Given a passage of Catholic liturgy, Scripture or commentary, retrieve passages relevant to this query\nQuery:{text}', prefix_doc: '', norm: 'l2' };
        ```
    - **Trigram retirement for ingest:** move `fnv1a`/`embedText`/`cosine`/`EMBED_DIM=128` verbatim from `src/core/vector/embed.ts:13-63` into `scripts/lib/trigram-embed.mjs`, which also re-exports `normalizeText` from `../../src/core/text/normalize.ts`. The three ingest imports become `from './lib/trigram-embed.mjs'`. This is idempotent: if BA.14 already did this with identical content, leave it.
    - **`scripts/embed-corpus.mjs`**, CLI `node --experimental-strip-types scripts/embed-corpus.mjs <input.db> [--out <dir>] [--restart]`. `<dir>` defaults to `build/corpus`, and the work file is `<dir>/embed-work.db`. `OLLAMA_HOST` defaults to `http://127.0.0.1:11434`.
      - Exit codes (Closed set, Phase L): 0 ok, 2 model/Ollama, 3 work-file mismatch, 4 invalid input.
      - Phases (Closed set, Phase L): `copy, vectors, centroids, instance_of, similar_to, probe, finalize`. They are recorded in `embed_progress(phase TEXT PRIMARY KEY, completed_at TEXT NOT NULL)` in the work file, and a completed phase is skipped on rerun.
      1. **Preflight.**
         - `POST /api/show {model:'qwen3-embedding:0.6b'}`. The `modelfile` line `^FROM (.+)$` must have basename `sha256-<artifact_sha256>`, and the streamed sha256 of that file must equal it.
         - `GET /api/version` → `embed_ollama_version`.
         - Any failure → exit 2, with a message naming OP.4.
      2. **`copy`.**
         - Open the input with `new DatabaseSync(path, { readOnly: true })`. A missing `nodes`/`text_blocks` → exit 4.
         - Work file absent: `VACUUM INTO` it, then create `embed_work(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)` with `input_sha256`, `corpus_version=<V>` and `started_at`.
           - `<V>` = `corpus-YYYY.MM.DD-HHMM-q3e256` (UTC, assigned once here).
         - Work file present with a different `input_sha256` → exit 3 (`work file belongs to another input; rerun with --restart`).
         - `--restart` renames the old work file to `embed-work.<started_at>.abandoned.db` (never deleted).
      3. **`vectors`.**
         - `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS embeddings_q3e256(node_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, dim INTEGER NOT NULL, vec BLOB NOT NULL)`.
         - Pending = `text_blocks` rows with non-empty `latin` or `english` and `node_id NOT IN embeddings_q3e256`, `ORDER BY node_id`.
         - docText = `[latin, english].filter(non-empty).join('\n\n')`, with no prefix.
         - Batches of 32: `POST /api/embed {model, input:[docText…], truncate:true, options:{num_ctx:8192}}`. Each vector must have length 1024, else exit 2.
         - Each vector is stored as: take `[0,256)`, L2-renormalize (`mag = sqrt(Σx²) || 1`), then `max(-127, min(127, round(x/mag·127)))`. This is the same formula as native `toStoredVector`.
         - **One transaction per batch.** A kill at any point resumes at the first missing node.
      4. **`centroids`** (one transaction):
         - Each `category='curated'` concept vector = the normalized int8 mean of its members' `embeddings_q3e256` vectors over existing INSTANCE_OF edges.
         - Each `category='auto'` concept likewise, over its trigram-cluster INSTANCE_OF members.
         - `CREATE TABLE edges_trigram_archive AS SELECT src.key src_key, dst.key dst_key, e.rel, e.weight, e.meta FROM edges e JOIN nodes src ON src.id=e.src JOIN nodes dst ON dst.id=e.dst WHERE e.rel='INSTANCE_OF' AND dst.category='auto'`. Delete those edges, **in the work file only**.
         - Write the centroid vectors into `embeddings_q3e256`.
         - Rename `embeddings` → `embeddings_trigram`, then `embeddings_q3e256` → `embeddings`.
      5. **`instance_of`** (one transaction):
         - `DELETE FROM edges WHERE rel='INSTANCE_OF' AND json_extract(meta,'$.method')='semantic'`.
         - For every node of kind `section|verse|commentary|quotation` with a vector, insert INSTANCE_OF → concept where cosine to the centroid ≥ 0.45, with weight = cosine and meta `{"method":"semantic"}`. Skip pairs that already have a non-semantic (curated) INSTANCE_OF edge.
      6. **`similar_to`:**
         - Track progress in `similar_done(node_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)`, one transaction per batch of 1000 source nodes, ordered by `node_id`.
         - Each source gets its top 8 neighbours with cosine ≥ 0.35. Order is cosine desc, then key asc. Weight = cosine and meta `{"method":"semantic"}`.
         - Concept nodes are excluded as source and as target, and a node never points to itself.
         - Universe: a module node (key `^(commentary:catena-aurea/|catena:|father:)`) ranks against all nodes. A base node ranks against base nodes only.
         - Use a normalized `Float32Array` N×256 matrix. `worker_threads` are allowed (`os.availableParallelism()-1`); the results must be deterministic.
      7. **`probe`:** write `content/library/embed-probe.json` per §2.8.
         - Strata, 5 each, base keys only: Mass `section:` not `section:Horas/`; Office `section:Horas/`; `verse:`; `commentary:haydock/`.
         - Candidates: docText of 200–1200 chars that also have a first `splitClauses` clause of 25–200 chars in their English (Latin if there is no English). Order by `sha256(key)` hex ascending and take the first 5 per stratum → `d01..d20`.
         - `qNN` = that clause, embedded with `prefix_query` (`{text}` replaced).
         - Item shape: `{id, role:'doc'|'query', key, text (without prefix), vec: base64 of 256 int8}`. Order `d01..d20` then `q01..q20`.
         - Top level: `{schema:1, corpusVersion:<V>, embedMeta:{7 keys}, ollamaVersion, items}`, 2-space JSON plus a newline.
      8. **`finalize`** (last), in one transaction:
         - `CREATE TABLE embed_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL)` with the 7 `RUNTIME_EMBED_META` rows.
         - `corpus_meta`: `corpus_version=<V>`, `derived_from=<input corpus_version>`, `embed_ollama_version`, `embed_completed_at`.
         - Insert `embed_progress('finalize')`.
         - Then `VACUUM INTO <dir>/missal.<V>.full.db`. If finalize is already recorded and the output exists, print the path and exit 0.
         - Also write `<dir>/MISSAL-DB-VERSIONS.entry.md`. Heading: `### <V> — semantic re-embed (Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B, 256-d) and Catena module split`. Bullets: derived from `<input version>`; vendored DO commit (`corpus_meta.vendored_commit`); Catena moved to module `catena-aurea-en` (R2), so the base no longer contains `commentary:catena-aurea/*`; trigram auto-concept edges archived in `edges_trigram_archive` (row count); counts of nodes, embeddings (dim 256), SIMILAR_TO and semantic INSTANCE_OF; the rollback line "prior corpus file untouched; revert the pointer commit".
    - **`package.json` scripts:**
      - `"corpus:ingest": "node -e \"require('node:fs').mkdirSync('build/corpus',{recursive:true})\" && node --experimental-strip-types scripts/ingest-corpus.mjs build/corpus/stage1-ingest.db"` (the caller sets `CORPUS_DESC`);
      - `"corpus:embed": "node --experimental-strip-types scripts/embed-corpus.mjs"` (input path after `--`).
    - **`DOCS/CORPUS-SCHEMA.md`:**
      - Replace the `embeddings(node_id, dim, vec)` bullet (`:96-98`) with: "`embeddings(node_id, dim, vec)` — 256-d Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B, first 256 of 1024 (Matryoshka), L2-renormalized, int8 ×127; identity in `embed_meta`. Pre-q3e256 files carry 128-d trigram."
      - Add the tables `embed_meta`, `edges_trigram_archive(src_key,dst_key,rel,weight,meta)` and `embed_progress(phase,completed_at)`; the rels `SIMILAR_TO`, `PART_OF`, `ATTRIBUTED_TO`, `CITES`; and the kinds `quotation`, `father` (module-only).
    - **Production run:** the same rule as BA.1. If BA.1 and BA.11 are both on master, run the full pipeline, prepend the printed entry under `## Entries` in `DOCS/MISSAL-DB-VERSIONS.md`, and commit it with `content/library/embed-probe.json` and `content/library/modules.json`.

    - **`scripts/catena-graph.mjs`:**
      - **`parseCatenaQuotations(raw: string): {quotations: {label, slug, locator: string|null, text, ordinal}[], skipped: number}`:**
        - **Attribution:** `ATTR := "**" LABEL "**."`, where LABEL is `[^*]+?` and may span a newline. It is normalized as above. A LABEL with no table match throws `UnknownFatherLabel(label)`.
        - **LOCATOR:** if the next non-space character is `(`, take the balanced-paren span (depth-counted, ≤ 200 chars) without the outer parens. Otherwise it is null.
        - **Text:** from the end of ATTR (plus LOCATOR) to the next ATTR or the pericope end, passed through `cleanMarkdown`. An empty text is skipped and counted, but still consumes its ordinal. `ordinal` is the 1-based attribution index within the pericope.
        - Text before the first ATTR stays only in the pericope.
      - **`CATENA_CITE_BOOKS`:** the §2.6 abbreviation map exactly. The excluded set is `Ps, Psalm, 1 Kings, 2 Kings, Infra, Luck`.
      - **`citesFor(text)`:** regex `/[\(\[]((?:[1-4] ?)?[A-Z][a-z]+)\.? ?(\d+):(\d+)(?:[-–](\d+))?/g`.
        - Normalize a digit prefix to `<d> <Name>` before the lookup.
        - Each match gives one target per existing `verse:<Key>/<ch>/<v>`, for v..v2 (range ≤ 30), deduped.
        - Unmapped, excluded, over-long or non-existent targets count toward `citesUnresolved`.
      - **`runCatenaGraph(input: string, output: string, opts?: {catenaDir?: string}): number`** returns the exit code (0 ok, 4 invalid input).
        1. Open `input` read-only. If `output` exists, rename it to `<output>.<UTC>.superseded.db` (never delete). Then `VACUUM INTO output`.
        2. In `output`, one transaction:
           - Insert all 51 father nodes: kind `father`, key `father:<slug>`, title, category `Catena`, meta `{aliases, class, moduleId:'catena-aurea-en'}`. No text, no vector.
           - Re-parse `opts.catenaDir ?? 'VENDORED/catena-aurea/catena-aurea-project/markdown_chapters'` with `parseCatena`. For each record, find the pericope node by key; for each quotation, insert:
             - **Node:** kind `quotation`, key `catena:<Book>/<ch>/<verseStart>/q<n>`, title `<Father title> on <Evangelist> <ch>:<verseStart>` (Evangelist map Matt→Matthew, Marc→Mark, Luc→Luke, Joann→John), category `Catena`, meta `{source:'catena-aurea', moduleId:'catena-aurea-en', father:<slug>, label, locator, ordinal:n, chapterFile, citesUnresolved:<int>}`.
             - **Text:** `text_blocks(section = the pericope's section string, latin NULL, english = text)`.
             - **Search:** `search(key, section, normalizeText(text))`.
             - **Edges:** `PART_OF` → pericope, `ATTRIBUTED_TO` → `father:<slug>`, `CITES` → each verse. All have weight 1.0 and meta null.
        3. An unknown label → `ROLLBACK`, print the label and file, and return 4.
        4. Print the census `{pericopes, attributions, quotations, skippedEmpty, fathers:51, cites, citesUnresolved}`.
      - **CLI:** at the bottom of `ingest-commentary.mjs`, guarded by `process.argv[1]?.endsWith('ingest-commentary.mjs') && process.argv[2] === '--catena-graph'`, dynamically import `./catena-graph.mjs` and set `process.exitCode = runCatenaGraph(process.argv[3], process.argv[4])`.
      - `package.json`: `"corpus:catena-graph": "node --experimental-strip-types scripts/ingest-commentary.mjs --catena-graph build/corpus/stage1-ingest.db build/corpus/stage2-graph.db"`.

    - `embed_load` in `spawn_blocking`: (1) file length ≠ `expected_bytes` → `Err("embed: artifact size mismatch")`; (2) `Some(expected_sha256)` → streamed sha256 via `sha2` (already a dependency), mismatch → `Err("embed: artifact digest mismatch")`; (3) `LlamaModel::load_from_file` with `LlamaModelParams::default()` (CPU-only, no GPU feature); (4) role check: `role` ∈ {`embed`,`rerank`} else `Err("embed: unknown role")`; `embed` requires `meta_val_str("qwen3.pooling_type") == "3"`, `rerank` requires `== "4"` and a present `tokenizer.chat_template.rerank` (stored as `rerank_template`), else `Err("embed: artifact role mismatch")`; (5) replace any existing session of the same role.
    - Sessions: `static EMBED_SESSIONS: Mutex<Option<HashMap<String, EmbedSession>>>`; `EmbedSession { model: LlamaModel, role: EmbedRole, rerank_template: Option<String> }` with the same `unsafe impl Send` justification comment as `inference.rs:58-64`; backend via `crate::inference::backend()`.
    - Per call: a fresh context (as `inference.rs:241`) with `with_n_ctx(NonZeroU32::new(2048))`, `with_n_batch(2048)`, `with_n_ubatch(2048)`, `with_n_threads(t)`, `with_n_threads_batch(t)` where `t = min(8, max(1, available_parallelism − 1))`, `with_embeddings(true)`, `with_pooling_type(LlamaPoolingType::Last)` (embed) or `LlamaPoolingType::Rank` (rerank), `with_type_k(KvCacheType::F16)`, `with_type_v(KvCacheType::F16)` (D2 "no turbo KV").
    - `embed_texts`: per text `str_to_token(text, AddBos::Always)`; if > 2048 tokens keep the first 2047 and push `model.token_eos()`; assert last token == `model.token_eos()` else `Err("embed: EOS not appended")`; `ctx.clear_kv_cache()`, `batch.add_sequence(&tokens, 0, false)`, `ctx.decode`, `ctx.embeddings_seq_ith(0)?.to_vec()` (1024 floats).
    - `rerank_scores`: per document, prompt = `rerank_template` with `{query}`/`{document}` replaced, tokenized with `AddBos::Never`; > 2048 tokens → `Err("embed: rerank prompt exceeds context")`; score = `embeddings_seq_ith(0)?[0]` (P(yes)).
    - Wrong-role session id → `Err("embed: session role mismatch")`; unknown id → `Err("embed: unknown session")`.

  - **Architect reconciliation (binding):** release §2.6/§2.7 make `public/` and `dist-web/` closed sets that fail on any `wllama*` file, and L.7-11e forbids wllama bytes in any artifact. Therefore the wllama wasm/js are **runtime assets**: vendored at `VENDORED/models/sha256/<d>/<fileName>`, mirrored at `https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/runtime/models/sha256/<d>/<fileName>` (OP.3), acquired into the OPFS model store (`WebModelLibrary`) by the preloader like the GGUFs, then handed to wllama as same-origin `blob:` URLs (wasm) and `{code}` (compat worker). This keeps the partial's S7 purposes — self-hosted (never jsDelivr), same-origin worker/wasm, offline after first download — and adds no `public/vendor/`, no Workbox runtime route, no COOP/COEP.

'native' : 'wllama'`, any rejection → `'wllama'` (target table: Linux/Windows/Android arm64 + x86_64 native; Android armeabi-v7a/x86 and web/PWA wllama); `export function embedderFor(runtime: EmbedRuntime, engines: { embed: IInferenceEngine; rerank: IInferenceEngine; embedSession: SessionId; rerankSession: SessionId | null }): { embedder: SemanticEmbedder; reranker: Reranker }` — one app-lifetime pair per runtime; `embedQuery(t)` = `toStoredVector((await engine.embed!(sid, [formatQuery(t)]))[0])`; `embedDocs(ts)` = each `capText(t)` (no prefix) → `toStoredVector`; `rerank(q, hits)` = `batchScore(sid, { items: hits.map(h => ({ context: [{ role: 'user', content: capText(q, MAX_RERANK_QUERY_CHARS) }], continuation: capText(h.text, MAX_RERANK_DOC_CHARS) })) })` → sort score desc, stable key-asc ties; all reject with `EmbedNotReadyError` until the respective state is `ready`. **Consumed contract for BA.14/BA.15 (exact):** `export type EmbedState = 'absent' | 'downloading' | 'ready' | 'error'`; `export function getEmbedder(): { state(): EmbedState; onState(l: (s: EmbedState) => void): () => void; embedQuery(t: string): Promise<Int8Array>; embedDocs(ts: readonly string[]): Promise<Int8Array[]> }`; `export function getReranker(): { state(): EmbedState; rerank(query: string, docs: string[]): Promise<number[]> }` — mapping ModelState → EmbedState: `absent|waiting-metered` → `'absent'`, `downloading|verifying|loading` → `'downloading'`, `ready` → `'ready'`, `error` → `'error'`; `getEmbedder()` delegates to the bound embedder (rejects `EmbedNotReadyError` before ready); `getReranker().rerank(query, docs)` returns scores **in input order** (calls the bound reranker with `key = String(index)` and scatters scores back by index); `onState` fires on every mapped-state change.

(1) `await after` (L.4 "after Supertonic"). (2) `runtime = await resolveEmbedRuntime(companionInvoke())`; write `runtime`. (3) Per model, embedder first then reranker, sequentially: **native on Android** (`navigator.userAgent` contains `Android`) — `model_pack_location({pack})`: `ready` → use `path`, pass sha to `embed_load` (`expectedSha256` set); `unsupported` → DownloadManager path below; otherwise `model_pack_fetch`, poll `model_pack_state` every 2 s (`waiting-wifi`/`requires-confirmation` → state `waiting-metered`; `downloading`/`transferring`/`pending` → `downloading` with progress `bytesDownloaded/totalBytes`; `failed`/`canceled`/`not-installed`/`unknown` → fall back to DownloadManager, mirror_url). **native on desktop** — `lib = makeLibrary(invoke, storageRootName())` (`src/core/chat/models.ts:97`, `src/core/storage/root.ts:64`), `lookup(asset)`; missing → `makeDownloadManager(lib).acquire(asset, mirrorUrl, asset.sha256, id)`; locator path → `embed_load` with `expectedSha256: null` (`model_finish` already proved the digest). **wllama** — always `new WebModelLibrary()` (OPFS, even inside Tauri); acquire the three `WLLAMA_ASSETS` rows then the GGUF the same way; wasm URL = `URL.createObjectURL(await lib.readFile(WLLAMA_ASSETS['wllama-3.8.1-wasm'].asset))`, compat wasm likewise, compat worker code = `await (await lib.readFile(…compat-js…)).text()`; `loadBlob = () => lib.readFile(pin.asset)`. DownloadProgress phases map `looking-up|downloading` → `downloading`, `verifying` → `verifying`; `progress` = Σreceived/Σtotal over every asset this run acquires. (4) **Metered rule:** before any network download, if `isMetered()` and no session consent → state `waiting-metered`; subscribe `onNetworkCostChange` and proceed when unmetered or when `consentMetered()` is called. Consent is session-only (module variable, never persisted — partial F4 governs over the audiobook's persisted per-group key for this group); on Android packs `consentMetered()` also calls `model_pack_confirm_cellular`. (5) Load: native → `new NativeEmbedEngine(invoke, pins, (op, d) => debugEvent('embed', op, d)).init({ modelId: id, artifactUrl: path, contextTokens: 2048 })`; wllama → `new WllamaEmbedEngine({...}).init({ modelId: id, artifactUrl: locator, contextTokens: 2048 })`; state `loading` during init, then `ready`; on both ready (or embedder ready + reranker error) `bindEmbedPair(embedderFor(runtime, …))`; emit `debugEvent('embed', 'load', {role, runtime, ms})`. (6) Failure: that model → `error` with `reason`; no automatic retry; embedder error ⇒ app stays on the D7 lexical fallback; reranker error ⇒ vector order.     - **`search.ts`.** All functions take `q: CorpusQuery = { sqlRows(sql: string, params?: unknown[]): Record<string, unknown>[]; vectorRows(): {key: string; section: string; vec: Int8Array}[] }`.
      - `type SearchMode = 'semantic'|'lexical'`.
      - `type GraphRel = 'SIMILAR_TO'|'ATTRIBUTED_TO'|'CITES'|'COMMENTS_ON'`.
      - `interface GraphNeighbour {key; title: string|null; rel: GraphRel; weight: number; direction: 'out'|'in'}`.
      - **`searchMode(q)`:** `'semantic'` iff `activeEmbedder()!==null` and `compatible(readEmbedMeta(q.sqlRows('SELECT key, value FROM embed_meta')), RUNTIME_EMBED_META)`. Missing table → lexical. Memoized per `q` in a WeakMap.
      - **`similarToText(q, text, k, excludeKey?): Promise<SimilarHit[]>`, semantic mode:**
        - `embedQuery(text)`; int8 cosine over `q.vectorRows()` minus `excludeKey`; take the top `max(k,20)`.
        - If the reranker is ready, rerank the first 20 with texts `english ?? latin`, reorder those 20 by rerank score, and set `hit.rerank`. The rest follow in cosine order.
        - Return the top k; `score` stays the cosine.
      - **`similarToText`, lexical mode:**
        - If `excludeKey` has outgoing `SIMILAR_TO` edges, return those, weight desc, with score = weight.
        - Otherwise use FTS: `normalizeText(text)` words of length ≥ 4, the first 12 distinct, each quoted, joined with ` OR `, then `SELECT key, section FROM search WHERE search MATCH ? ORDER BY rank LIMIT ?`. Score `(k−i)/k`. `excludeKey` is removed. A malformed query → `[]`.
        - Hits carry title/latin/english exactly as `corpusDb.ts:775-788`.
      - **`nucleusSources(q)`** = `['haydock', ...SELECT nucleus_source FROM module_attach]` (absent table → `['haydock']`).
      - **`interpretiveNucleiForText(q, text, opts)`:** the logic of `corpusDb.ts:471-542`, with the query vector from `embedQuery` and clauses from `bestClauses`. Default sources are `nucleusSources(q)`. Lexical → `[]`.
      - **`nucleatedSimilarToText(q, text, opts)`:** grouping, completeness and `contextScore = 0.7·hit.score + 0.3·nucleusAffinity` identical to `corpusDb.ts:549-635`.
        - Affinity = cosine of the cached clause vectors, from one `bestClauses` batch over every candidate and every nucleus.
        - The concept fallback uses a copy of `conceptsForSectionKey`'s SQL (`corpusDb.ts:895`).
        - Lexical: affinity 0.
      - **`conceptsForText(q, text, k)`:** semantic → cosine to concept vectors (dim 256); lexical → `[]`.
      - **`graphNeighbours(q, key, k): GraphNeighbour[]`:**
        - Outgoing edges with rel ∈ GraphRel, plus incoming `COMMENTS_ON`/`CITES`/`ATTRIBUTED_TO`.
        - Self excluded; deduped by key, keeping the max weight; ordered weight desc, then key asc; top k.
        - Works in both modes.
    - **`clause.ts`:**

      - `ClauseVectorCache`: an LRU of 8192, keyed by `normalizeText(clause)`, filled by **one** `embedDocs` call per query for all missing clauses. The argmax keeps the first clause on ties.
      - Lexical (no active embedder): the clause with the most shared normalized words wins (ties: first), with score 0.

    - `voice.ts` exports the amended §K.4 contract verbatim from the partial §2, plus the option types the engine and runtime code against:
      ```ts
      export type VoiceBackend = 'webgpu' | 'wasm';                         // Closed set, Phase K
      export type SynthPriority = 'interactive' | 'background';             // Closed set, Phase L
      export interface SynthesizedAudio { pcm: Float32Array; sampleRate: number; durationSec: number }
      export interface Voice {
        backend: VoiceBackend;
        speak(text: string, opts: { signal?: AbortSignal }): Promise<void>;  // K.4, unchanged
        synthesize(text: string, opts: { signal?: AbortSignal; priority?: SynthPriority }): Promise<SynthesizedAudio>;
        stop(): void;
        dispose(): Promise<void>;
      }
      export interface Calibration { backend: VoiceBackend; threads: number; synthWorkers: number; ortThreadsPerWorker: number }
      export interface VoiceAssetFile { fileName: string; sha256: string; file: Blob }
      export interface SupertonicVoiceOptions { assets: readonly VoiceAssetFile[]; calibration: Calibration }
      ```
      The `synthesize` doc comment states the contract exactly: input is exactly one sentence (as produced by `segmentSentences` or the audiobook sub-split) phonemized whole with punctuation retained; output mono PCM in [-1, 1] at the engine's native rate; `sampleRate` is read from the Supertonic config and is never a literal; `durationSec = pcm.length / sampleRate`; it never touches an AudioContext and never plays; on `signal` abort it rejects with `new DOMException('Aborted', 'AbortError')` and frees its pool slot; `priority` defaults to `'background'`; `speak()` uses `'interactive'` internally; concurrent calls are safe (served by `SynthesisPool`, TT.2).
    - `sentences.ts` exports `segmentSentences(text: string): string[]` and `normalizeWs(s: string): string` (= `s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()`). Whole-text scan; a boundary is one of `. ! ? …` followed by optional closing quotes/brackets `[”’"')\]»]*` and then whitespace or end of text. No split when the token ending in `.` is in the table-driven `ABBREVIATIONS` (exported `const`, Closed set, Phase K): `Mr. Mrs. Dr. St. Sts. Ss. Bl. Fr. Rev. Ven. vs. etc. cf. ch. v. vv. i.e. e.g. A.D. B.C. viz. Lib.`, and never between two digits (`3.5`). Empty or whitespace-only input returns `[]`; no element is ever empty. **Anti-drop invariant (K.4):** `normalizeWs(segmentSentences(t).join(' ')) === normalizeWs(t)` for every input.

Engine: Supertonic as deployed at natally.surge.sh (engine provenance only, never fetched); structural reference natally v1.32 `apps/local/src/voice/web.ts` (ORT session input validation, WebGPU→WASM probe, no CDN, no browser speech).

  - **Do:**
    - **`calibrate.ts`** — `calibrate(): Promise<Calibration>`, memoized for the process, run once at startup:
      - `backend = (await navigator.gpu?.requestAdapter?.()) ? 'webgpu' : 'wasm'` (any throw → `'wasm'`).
      - `threads = max(1, min((navigator.hardwareConcurrency ?? 2) − 1, 8))`.
      - `memCap = navigator.deviceMemory ? max(1, floor(navigator.deviceMemory / 2)) : 2`.
      - `synthWorkers = backend === 'webgpu' ? 1 : clamp(min(threads, 4, memCap), 1, 4)`.
      - `ortThreadsPerWorker = crossOriginIsolated ? max(1, floor(threads / synthWorkers)) : 1`.
      - Emits exactly one `debugEvent('voice', 'calibrate', {backend, threads, synthWorkers, ortThreadsPerWorker})` per launch. rtf is **not** measured here (S6).
    - **`assets.ts`** — `export interface VoiceAssetRow { id: string; fileName: string; sha256: string; sizeBytes: number; mirrorUrl: string }`; `export const VOICE_ASSETS: readonly VoiceAssetRow[]` between the markers `// <voice-assets:generated>` and `// </voice-assets:generated>`, written only by `scripts/voice-assets.mjs --sync`. Until OP.6 has run it is `[]` (graceful: the runtime reports voice `error`, BA.7). `mirrorUrl` = `https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/runtime/models/sha256/<sha256>/<fileName>` (R3 runtime host; the upstream URL is provenance only, never fetched). `voiceKey(rows = VOICE_ASSETS): Promise<string>` = first 16 hex chars of `sha256(rows.map(r => r.sha256).sort().join('\n'))` via `crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', …)`. `voiceDownloadMB(rows = VOICE_ASSETS): number` = `Math.round(Σ sizeBytes / 1048576 / 10) * 10`.
    - **`scripts/voice-assets.mjs`** (< 500 lines, Node only):
      - `--add <file> --upstream <url> --revision <rev> --license <spdx>`: computes sha256 + size; copies to `VENDORED/models/sha256/<sha256>/<basename>` (skip when identical bytes already exist); upserts one row in `VENDORED/models/manifest.json` (creating `{"schema":"sam.runtime-assets.v1","host":"https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba","assets":[]}` if absent) with `{id:'supertonic-'+<basename without extension, lowercased, non-[a-z0-9] → '-'>, kind:'supertonic', fileName, mirror_url:'/runtime/models/sha256/<sha256>/<fileName>', sha256, size_bytes, upstream_url_of_record, upstream_revision, license, mirrored_at:null, android_pack:null, runtime_path:null}`; appends one line to `VENDORED/models/PROVENANCE.md` (sha256, size, upstream URL @ revision, UTC date). Rows of any other `kind` are never touched.
      - `--sync`: rewrites only the generated block of `src/core/voice/assets.ts` from the manifest's `kind:'supertonic'` rows, sorted by `fileName`.
      - `--check`: side-effect free; exits 1 when the generated block differs from the manifest rows or a vendored file's sha256/size differs from its row; exits 0 otherwise (including zero rows).
    - **`synth.worker.ts`** — module Worker created with `new Worker(new URL('./synth.worker.ts', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })`. Messages (Closed set, Phase L): in `{type:'init', assets:{fileName:string; buffer:ArrayBuffer}[], backend, ortThreads}` → out `{type:'ready', sampleRate:number}` | `{type:'error', message:string}`; in `{type:'synth', id:number, text:string}` → out `{type:'done', id, pcm:Float32Array /*transferred*/, sampleRate}` | `{type:'failed', id, message}`. Imports `onnxruntime-web/webgpu` when `backend==='webgpu'`, else `onnxruntime-web/wasm`; sets `ort.env.wasm.numThreads = ortThreads` and `ort.env.wasm.wasmPaths` to same-origin URLs from Vite `?url` imports of the package's `dist/ort-wasm-simd-threaded*.{wasm,mjs}` files — never a CDN. Keeps the original TT.2 engine discipline: one `InferenceSession` per `.onnx` asset, session input/output names validated at init (v1.32 discipline; mismatch → `error`), whole-sentence phonemization with the `phonemizer` package (eSpeak-NG, punctuation retained), tensors fed per the deployed Supertonic model contract, `sampleRate` read from the voice config asset (never a literal).
    - **`pool.ts`** — `class SynthesisPool { constructor(n: number, ortThreads: number, assets: readonly VoiceAssetFile[]); ready(): Promise<{ sampleRate: number }>; run(text: string, opts: { signal?: AbortSignal; priority?: SynthPriority }): Promise<SynthesizedAudio>; abortAll(): void; dispose(): Promise<void> }`. The backend comes from the memoized `calibrate()`. Spawns `n` workers, each sent the asset bytes (`await file.arrayBuffer()` per worker). Two FIFO lanes; a free worker always dequeues `'interactive'` before `'background'`; one job per worker. Abort before dispatch → remove from lane and reject `AbortError`; abort in flight → reject `AbortError` at once, discard the worker's later result, and mark the slot free when that result arrives. Interactive jobs pre-empt only by priority; they never abort background jobs. `abortAll()` rejects every queued and in-flight job with `AbortError`. `dispose()` terminates all workers.
    - **`supertonic.ts`** — `createSupertonicVoice(options: SupertonicVoiceOptions): Promise<Voice>`: builds `new SynthesisPool(options.calibration.synthWorkers, options.calibration.ortThreadsPerWorker, options.assets)` and awaits `ready()` (any worker init failure rejects create). `synthesize(text, {signal, priority})` → `pool.run(text, {signal, priority: priority ?? 'background'})`, returning `{pcm, sampleRate, durationSec: pcm.length / sampleRate}`. `speak(text, {signal})` → `new SpeakingPipeline(voice).speak(text, {signal})` (the pipeline calls `synthesize` with `'interactive'`); `stop()` stops the active pipeline; `dispose()` stops it and disposes the pool. No `speechSynthesis`, no CDN, no same-origin `/voice/` path.

Files: `src/core/voice/pipeline.ts` (new), `src/core/voice/worklet.ts` (new — worklet source string, v1.32 pattern). No `tests/voicePipeline.test.ts` (W13). **Scope (S2/S3):** Sacristan replies and reading-view speech only. The audiobook never uses this pipeline or the worklet (it uses BA.7's RenderQueue and HTMLAudioElement pair).
  - **Consumes:** TT.1 `Voice` (only `synthesize`), `segmentSentences(text): string[]`; `debugEvent` (`src/core/diagnostics/store.ts:60`).
  - **Do:**
    - Exports (exact):
      ```ts
      export interface SentenceEvent { sentenceIndex: number; sentence: string; phase: 'started' | 'ended' }   // phase: Closed set, Phase K
      export interface SpokenEvent extends SentenceEvent { text: string }   // text = the exact string passed to speak()
      export class SpeakingPipeline {
        constructor(voice: Pick<Voice, 'synthesize'>);
        speak(text: string, opts: { signal?: AbortSignal }): Promise<void>;
        stop(): void;
        on(type: 'sentence', l: (e: SentenceEvent) => void): () => void;
        on(type: 'frame', l: (e: { level: number }) => void): () => void;
      }
      export function subscribeSpoken(l: (e: SpokenEvent) => void): () => void;   // module-level bus fed by every pipeline
      ```
    - `speak` segments with `segmentSentences(text)`; synthesis goes through `voice.synthesize(sentence, { signal, priority: 'interactive' })` — the TT.2 `SynthesisPool` provides concurrency, so the pipeline owns no workers. **Lookahead:** synthesis of sentence N+1 is dispatched when playback of N starts; at most two synthesis jobs are in flight.
    - Playback through the AudioWorklet in `worklet.ts` (v1.32 pattern): resample `sampleRate` → context rate; 20 ms RMS envelope frames posted as `{type:'frame', level}` and re-emitted as `frame` events.
    - Sentence boundaries emit `{sentenceIndex, sentence, phase:'started'}` / `{…, phase:'ended'}` on the instance and on `subscribeSpoken` (with `text`).
    - `stop()` (or `signal` abort) aborts synthesis and playback atomically and resolves `speak`. A synthesis failure records `debugEvent('voice', 'pipeline-skip', {sentenceIndex, error}, 'warn')` and skips to the next sentence — never stalls. No word is dropped at boundaries: each sentence is phonemized from the whole sentence (TT.1 invariant).

Steps 0–3 need no code; steps 4–5 run once BA.12 (`scripts/mirror-model-assets.mjs`) is landed, and the modules part once the module build/deploy tasks have produced files.
  - **Step 0 — Ubuntu build host toolchain (D11):** `sudo apt install clang-19 lld-19 llvm-19` (candidate `1:19.1.1-1ubuntu1~24.04.2`); `/usr/lib/llvm-19/bin/clang-cl --version` prints `clang version 19.`. Do not edit `/usr/local/bin/clang-cl` (BA.12's script prepends `/usr/lib/llvm-19/bin` to PATH).
  - **Step 1 — server root (CT123):** `mkdir -p /var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime/models/sha256 /var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime/modules`, writable by the SSH user named in step 3; outside the swapped `current` symlink so files survive web remounts.
  - **Step 2 — nginx** (inside the existing `sanctissimissa.robin.mba` server block; mirror the file into `Admin-Manual/SERVERS/nginx-ui/sites-available/`), union of release §2.14 and native §2.4:
    ```
    location /runtime/ {
      alias /var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime/;
      if ($request_method = OPTIONS) { add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always; add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Range" always; add_header Access-Control-Max-Age 86400 always; return 204; }
      add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always;
      add_header Access-Control-Allow-Headers "Range" always;
      add_header Access-Control-Expose-Headers "Content-Length, Content-Range, Accept-Ranges, ETag" always;
      add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" always;
      types { application/wasm wasm; application/javascript js; application/octet-stream gguf db onnx bin; application/json json; }
    }
    location = /runtime/models/manifest.json { alias /var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime/models/manifest.json; add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always; add_header Cache-Control "no-cache" always; }
    location = /runtime/modules/modules.json { alias /var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime/modules/modules.json; add_header Access-Control-Allow-Origin "*" always; add_header Cache-Control "no-cache" always; }
    ```
    `nginx -t && systemctl reload nginx`. Static files keep nginx's default `Accept-Ranges: bytes`.
  - **Step 3 — transport env:** in `.env` (keys only, no values, also appear in `.env.example` via BA.9): `SANCTISSIMISSA_RUNTIME_SSH=<user@host that can write the root>`, `SANCTISSIMISSA_RUNTIME_ROOT=/var/www/sanctissimissa/runtime`. Mirror the credential pointer into Admin-Manual CREDENTIALS.

  - **Step 5 — verify:** `node --experimental-strip-types scripts/mirror-model-assets.mjs --verify-remote` exits 0 (every row 200, sha256 + size equal, `access-control-allow-origin: *`) and writes `mirrored_at` into `VENDORED/models/manifest.json`; commit that file alone (`git push --no-verify origin master`; never contact Forgejo).

**commerce authors:**

Code facts that the partial does not state and that the tasks resolve (please confirm at stanza review):
1. Kotlin version: purchases-android 11.2.0 requires Kotlin 2.1.0+ (DOCS/sdk/purchases-android/installation-android.md:33), but src-tauri/gen/android/build.gradle.kts:8 pins kotlin-gradle-plugin 1.9.25. BA.4 adds the bump to 2.1.0 (an extra file beyond the partial's list).
2. Rust error shape: in Tauri 2.11.5, ErrorResponse.message is Option<String>. The partial's `format!("{}: {}", e.code.unwrap_or("UNKNOWN"), e.message)` would not compile. BA.4 specifies `e.code.unwrap_or_else(|| "UNKNOWN".into())` and `e.message.unwrap_or_default()`. run_mobile_plugin and PluginInvokeError exist only under cfg(mobile), so the Android bodies and the Commerce struct are cfg-gated.
3. Android acceptance: the partial's check "unzip -l of base.apk shows com/revenuecat/purchases classes" can't work, because classes are compiled into dex. BA.4 checks the dex strings and the BILLING permission instead.
4. NucleusSourceManifest name collision: an unused NucleusSourceManifest already exists at src/core/data/types.ts:90 with a different shape (id/label/sha256/moduleId). The content-modules partial §2.9 uses that shape inside module_meta; the commerce partial defines a new one in src/core/library/nucleus.ts. BA.3 follows the commerce partial and leaves the old type alone. The architect should reconcile the two shapes.

Its checksumSha256 is a reproducible digest of the vendored .sfm files; the formula is stated in the task. The partial names the data source but not the checksum basis.

**reader-ui authors:**

I wrote BA.6 only. Its files are listed in the task.

I added no operator tasks: everything BA.6 needs from outside comes from BA.1–BA.3, BA.7, BA.8 and BA.14, and BA.6 runs without them, just with less on screen.

Seven partial conflicts or gaps need an architect ruling. I resolved each as follows.

1. **Concept card id.** The commerce partial names it `#concept-surfacing`; the audiobook partial E19 assigns `#concept-surfacing-card`. I used `#concept-surfacing-card`, because the audiobook partial explicitly claims the id and lists it in its closed set of new ids. The commerce partial still governs the card's content.
2. **Concept-card TTS buttons.** Commerce §4.3 lists `Preview TTS` / `Play TTS` as not mounted. Audiobook E19 mounts them only for attached `AUDIOBOOK_EDITIONS` hits. I followed the more specific E19 rule.
3. **`LibraryReader` signature.** The partial gives `(LibraryViewProps & {editionId})`, but its own binding needs `← My Books` to return to My Books. I widened it with `onBack: () => void`, and `MyBooksPanel` hosts the reader.
4. **Catalogue Read gap.** Nothing in the partials defines how the Catalogue drawer's Read (`#primary-action-btn`, BA.5) opens the reader. Suggestion: BA.5 switches to the My Books tab and `MyBooksPanel` gains an optional `openEditionId` prop, or a closed window event. This needs a ruling.
5. **`installAndAttach` signature.** It is unspecified, so the Download action composes `installer.install` + `installer.load` + `db.attachModule` instead.
6. **Sync badge.** There is no per-edition inflight accessor on `AudiobookController`, so `☁ Preparing audio…` uses `storedCount < sentenceCount` on the open edition as a proxy.
7. **Two notes counts.** The My Books `📝 N Notes` badge counts sidecar accompaniments anchored `book:<id>#…`, while the reader Notes tab counts `annotationsFor` (localStorage, keyed by pericope). They can differ until annotations move to the sidecar.

Other decisions:
- **Haydock inspector.** Haydock has no `EditionRecord`, so the inspector shows the card's edition line and omits `#drawer-licence` rather than inventing copy.
- **Helper and CSS files.** I added `progress.ts`, `EditionInspector.tsx`, `ConceptSurfacing.tsx`, `ReaderNotebook.tsx`, `mybooks.css` and `reader.css` as splits to keep the line caps and carry the export styles. No partial names them.
- **Where the dependencies sit.**
  - `moduleKeys.ts` belongs to BA.6, per the content-modules entity row.
  - `editionSections` depends on BA.14's public `CorpusDb.sqlRows`.
  - `TEST_RUBRIC.md` has no L.7-n rows yet; BA.9 commits them in the plan phase.

The signed-off partials contradict each other in several places. To keep the tasks parallel and avoid two groups writing the same file, I had to pick a side each time. These choices should be confirmed with the operator or recorded as an addendum.

1. **Corpus pointer and release inputs** (content §2.1/§3.1(d)/§3.2 vs release §2.6/§2.12/§4).
   - **Conflict:**
     - Content puts the pointer at `assets/corpus.current.json`, with `scripts/corpus-current.mjs`, `corpus:restore`, `build.rs` `SAM_CORPUS_PATH`/`lib.rs include_bytes!(env!)`, and edits to `sync-db.mjs`, `release-state.mjs:234`, `collect-artifacts.mjs`, `verify-calendar.mjs` and `db-adapter.mjs`. It also has BA.1 write `modules-deploy.mjs` with rsync.
     - Release puts the pointer at `VENDORED/corpus/CURRENT.json` (`release-inputs.mjs --vendor`), keeps `sync-db.mjs` materializing `assets/missal.db` with `build.rs` unchanged, and gives `modules-deploy.mjs` (scp + `mv -n`) to BA.9.
   - **Chosen:** release governs the pointer, vendoring, sync-db, restore, deploy and dist staging. BA.1 writes none of those files. Its outputs take `--out <dir>` so BA.9's `--pipeline`/`--vendor` can consume them.
   - **Not written as a result:** the content §4 rows "corpus pointer + resolver", "build.rs corpus path", "corpus-restore.mjs" and the `modules-deploy` half of "modules-stage, modules-deploy". BA.1 keeps `modules-stage.mjs`, which writes the same `dist/` names as release §2.4 with equal-bytes reuse, plus the Play-pack copy no other task performs.

2. **embed_meta `model` value and constant location.**
   - **Conflict:** content §2.2 has `model`=`qwen3-embedding:0.6b` in `RUNTIME_EMBED_META` (`src/core/vector/meta.ts`). Native §2.2 has `CURRENT_EMBED_META` in `embedFormat.ts` with `model`=`'qwen3-embedding-0.6b-q8_0'`, "single source of truth".
   - **Chosen:** BA.10/BA.14 use content's value in `meta.ts`. The corpus format is content-owned, and `compatible()` needs one value.
   - **Needed:** BA.13's `CURRENT_EMBED_META` must equal it; ideally `embedFormat.ts` re-exports `RUNTIME_EMBED_META`. Otherwise every module attach and `searchMode` is incompatible.

3. **Embedder API names.**
   - **Conflict:** content §2.11 consumes `getEmbedder()/state()/onState`, `getReranker().rerank(query, string[]) → number[]`. Native defines `embedderFor(runtime)`, the `preload.ts` readiness API (`embedReadiness`/`subscribeEmbedReadiness`/`isEmbedReady`/`isRerankReady`), and `Reranker.rerank(query, {key,text}[]) → {key,score}[]`.
   - **Chosen:** BA.14 codes against native's actual exports through a small new `src/core/vector/active.ts`.

4. **npm script names.** Content uses `corpus:ingest`/`corpus:catena-graph`/`corpus:embed`/`corpus:modules`; release uses `embed:corpus`/`build:modules`/`ingest:commentary`/`corpus:pipeline`. I kept content's names (different keys, so no file collision). Release's `embed:corpus`/`build:modules` lack `--experimental-strip-types`, which these scripts need because they import `.ts` (BA.9 should add it). `corpus:restore` is dropped.

5. **`corpus_version` grammar.** Content uses `corpus-YYYY.MM.DD-HHMM-q3e256`; release's `CURRENT.json` example and F1 use `corpus-YYYY.MM.DD-HHMM`. BA.9's `release-inputs --verify` must accept the `-q3e256` suffix.

6. **Vendored module file name.** Release §2.6 (`sanctissimissa-module-<id>-r<N>.db`) disagrees with §2.4 and `modules.json` `file` (`catena-aurea-en-r<N>.db`). BA.1's `modules-stage` accepts either source name.

7. **Task ordering.** Native §4 "BA.12 → BA.13 → BA.14; BA.10 imports embedFormat.ts" contradicts the Architecture Law. The tasks are made order-independent instead:
   - BA.10 does not import `embedFormat` and inlines the identical int8 formula.
   - BA.10 and BA.14 share an identical create-if-absent `meta.ts` block and `trigram-embed.mjs`.
   - Unlanded cross-task imports are allowed only TS2307, confined to one file (`moduleBoot.ts`, `active.ts`, `retrieval.ts`).
   - The full corpus pipeline is run and committed by whichever of BA.1/BA.10/BA.11 lands last.
   - BA.14's interim ChatView async fix applies only if BA.15 hasn't landed.

8. **Shared files.** These merge rules are spelled out in the tasks:
   - `vite.config.ts` `globIgnores`: union with BA.13.
   - `lib.rs`, `settings.gradle`, `app/build.gradle.kts`, `build:android` and the android-release stage: token-local additions that coexist with BA.12.
   - `corpusDb.ts`: BA.2 adds 3 lines; BA.14 changes the search methods. The cache field name `vectors` is shared.

9. **Small additions not named in the content partial**, needed to make the specified contracts implementable. All are additive:
   - `AttachRefusedError` class (core.ts);
   - `ModuleInstaller.installed()` and the `moduleInstaller` singleton (for BA.6's "Update available");
   - `bootModules(db, sidecar)`, which wires `getCommerce` (BA.3 `service.ts`);
   - `startParityProbe()`;
   - `accompanimentEmbedText()` export;
   - quotation `meta.citesUnresolved` (so BA.1 can compute `module_meta.cites_unresolved` from data);
   - `parseCatena` record field `raw`;
   - `SacristanContextItem`/`SacristanSource` live in BA.15's `retrieval.ts`, not `search.ts`;
   - `liveFacts` moved into `systemContext.ts`.

**native-engine authors:**

I wrote BA.12, BA.13 and OP.3. There is no BA.16, because the native-embed partial says "No BA.16 is needed". I edited no files.

**Conflicts between partials and how the tasks resolve them**

1. **Runtime host prefix.**
   - The conflict: native-embed §2.4 puts the mirror at https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/models/sha256/… and /vendor/wllama/…. The release partial §2.14 and the standing rule say https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/runtime/.
   - The tasks use `/runtime/models/sha256/<d>/<file>`. The manifest is published at `/runtime/models/manifest.json`, and the manifest `host` field is "https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/runtime".

2. **Where the wllama wasm is served.**
   - The conflict: native-embed F6/S7 puts the files in `public/vendor/wllama/3.8.1/` and bundles them into dist-web. Release §2.6 makes `public/` a closed set (icon.png, icon-192.png, missal.db). Release §2.7 makes the `web` stage fail on any `wllama*` file in dist-web, and rubric row L.7-11e forbids wllama bytes in every artifact. Following the native partial as written would fail the release gauntlet.
   - The tasks treat the three wllama files as runtime assets:
     - they are vendored under `VENDORED/models/sha256/<d>/` (release §2.6 already lists the wllama wasm there);
     - they are mirrored on /runtime/;
     - they are downloaded into OPFS by the preloader;
     - they are handed to wllama as a `blob:` URL (wasm) and as `{code}` for the compat worker (`WllamaCompat.worker` accepts `{code: string}` per DOCS/sdk/wllama-3.8.1/wllama.d.ts.md).
   - This keeps S7's stated purposes: self-hosted (never jsDelivr), same-origin worker and wasm, and offline use after the first download.
   - Consequences:
     - `vendor-wllama.mjs` copies into VENDORED, not `public/`, and is not chained into predev or prebuild.
     - The CacheFirst Workbox route for wllama is dropped.
     - `runtime_path` is null on all rows.
   - The orchestrator may want to ratify this in a ledger note.

3. **Metered-download consent.**
   - The conflict: the audiobook partial §2.6 owns `src/core/net/metered.ts` (`networkCost`, `onNetworkCostChange`, `mayDownload`, `grantMeteredConsent`, with consent persisted per group and a group named 'embed-models'). Native-embed F4 asks for `isMetered()` and session-only consent.
   - BA.13 creates `metered.ts` with only the BA.7 §2.6 `networkCost`/`onNetworkCostChange` contract if the file is absent, and appends `isMetered()`.
   - Consent for the search models stays session-only, because the native partial owns ModelPreloader.

4. **nginx cache headers.**
   - The conflict: the release partial's nginx block sets `immutable` on all of /runtime/, but `models/manifest.json` and `modules/modules.json` are the mutable files.
   - OP.3 adds exact-match `no-cache` locations for those two files. It also adds Range to `Access-Control-Allow-Headers`, the native §2.4 `Access-Control-Expose-Headers`, and an OPTIONS 204 for preflight.

**Making the tasks order-independent**

- BA.12 and BA.13 each carry the identical full text of the shared files `src/core/vector/assets.ts` and `VENDORED/models/manifest.json`, under a create-if-absent, verify-if-present rule.
- BA.13 codes against BA.12's `NativeEmbedEngine` and Tauri command signatures. If BA.12 has not landed, `embed_probe` rejects and the runtime falls back to wllama.
- `EmbedRole` lives in `assets.ts`, so that file has no dependency on BA.13.
- A new `embedState.ts` breaks the import cycle between `preload.ts` and `embedder.ts`.
- `NativeEmbedEngine` and `WllamaEmbedEngine` take an injected `onEvent` callback, so `reusable-chatbot/` never imports from `src/`.
- `getEmbedder()`/`getReranker()` match the content partial §2.11 exactly. `getReranker().rerank` returns scores in input order, because BA.14 reorders them itself.
- The voice-init promise comes from BA.7's `settled()` in `src/core/voice/runtime.ts`, which does not exist yet. App wiring uses it if it exists at code time, otherwise `Promise.resolve()`.

**Smaller discrepancies, flagged but not acted on**

- The content partial says device documents are truncated at 512 tokens. The native partial (the owner) caps at 2048 tokens and 4000 code points. The tasks keep the native caps.
- The content partial names `RUNTIME_EMBED_META` in `meta.ts`, owned by BA.14. The native partial's `CURRENT_EMBED_META` in `embedFormat.ts`, owned by BA.13, should be its source.
- The `DOCS/sdk/llama-cpp-2` snapshot is 0.1.156, but the pin is now =0.1.154. The partial asserts the cited setters exist in 0.1.154. A re-snapshot at 0.1.154 would satisfy TC7 strictly.
- Rubric row L.7-11e's wording ("no *.gguf … in every asset pack") contradicts both the native F3 GGUF packs and the content §2.5 paid Catena module pack. The tasks read it as "only the declared packs may carry runtime bytes". The release partial should clarify this.
- `clang-19` is not installed on this host (`/usr/lib/llvm-19` is absent), so I put the apt install in OP.3 as step 0. The Windows cross-build check in BA.12 is recorded as pending-operator until then.

**Risk the orchestrator should weigh: GitHub LFS capacity**

The two GGUFs total about 1.28 GB, and the wllama files add about 25 MB. Per `.gitattributes`, LFS was formerly on Forgejo, and Forgejo was removed on 2026-11-06. BA.12 says what to do if GitHub rejects the LFS upload: untrack the GGUFs, note it in the commit message, and `--check` then exits 3 on a clean clone. The bytes still reach devices through the OP.3 mirror. A separate operator task may be warranted to confirm GitHub LFS quota or name another LFS store. I did not add one, to avoid colliding with OP ids that other groups assign.

**audiobook authors:**

I wrote BA.7, BA.8, amended TT.1–TT.4 (marked amended 2026-11-06, §L.9) and two operator tasks, OP.6 and OP.7. The OP numbers are placeholders; the orchestrator should renumber them to fit the global OP sequence. I edited no files.

Gaps and conflicts in the frozen partials, and how each task handles them:
1. **ModuleInstaller has no revision getter.** The audiobook partial says the ChunkStore `<revision>` comes from the installed revision as recorded by ModuleInstaller. The content-modules partial's ModuleInstaller only exposes `catalogue/install/load/remove`. BA.7 adds `revision.ts`, which reads the installer's IndexedDB record (`sanctissimissa.library.packages.v1`/`packages`) read-only. It uses the existing abort-on-upgrade pattern so the database is never created. If there is no record it uses `0`, so the audiobook still works while Catena rows are still in the base corpus. The `installer` dependency of `initAudiobook` is dropped as a result. Its `sidecar` dependency becomes a getter `() => SidecarDb | null`, because App opens the sidecar asynchronously and its state stays null until then.
2. **Runtime path conflict.** native-embed §2.4 writes `mirror_url` as `/models/sha256/...` at the host root. The release partial §2.14 says every runtime-asset row starts with `https://sanctissimissa.robin.mba/runtime/`. The voice rows use `/runtime/models/sha256/<sha>/<file>`, following the release partial. The native-embed rows need reconciling.
3. **Two metered-consent rules.** native-embed F4 names `isMetered()` and says consent lasts for the session and is not persisted. The audiobook partial persists `downloads.metered.<group>`. BA.7's `metered.ts` follows the audiobook partial and also exports `isMetered()`. BA.13 should adopt `mayDownload`/`grantMeteredConsent`, or the partials should state which rule wins.
4. **No Supertonic manifest rows exist yet.** The audiobook partial authors no `kind:'supertonic'` rows and `VENDORED/models/` does not exist. TT.2 adds `scripts/voice-assets.mjs` with `--add`, `--sync` and `--check`, plus a generated `VOICE_ASSETS` block. OP.6 is the operator supplying the bytes, and OP.7 mirrors them. No Android asset pack is declared for voice, so Android downloads into the webview's OPFS through DownloadManager.
5. **No speak trigger for Sacristan replies.** No existing task wires one, yet K.4's scope and acceptance L.7-6c assume replies are spoken. Amended TT.4 adds `useReplySpeech`: a reply finishes streaming, and it is spoken when the voice is ready. Please confirm. A mute toggle, and a reading-view speak trigger in SectionReader, remain unowned. TT.4 mounts only the highlight in the reader.
6. **Audio focus needs a reader.** The partial's VoiceRuntime has `claimAudio` but nothing to read the current owner. BA.7 adds `audioOwner()`. It also adds `onAudiobook(l)` and `useAudiobookBoot`, so App.tsx (771 lines) gains only 2 lines per task.
7. **Two small signature changes.** `ChunkStore.summary` gains `(fromChapterKey, order)` so `readyThrough` can be computed. `AudiobookPlayer` gains a `getChunk` constructor dependency.
8. **Concept card conflict (BA.6's concern).** The bookstore-commerce partial names the concept card id `#concept-surfacing` and says Preview/Play TTS are not mounted. The audiobook partial's E19 says `#concept-surfacing-card` with those buttons mounted. This needs resolving.
9. **Dock mount ownership.** BA.8 adds the dock mounts to MyBooksPanel and LibraryReader only if those BA.6 files exist when it lands; otherwise BA.6 mounts the dock against the stated signature.
10. **TEST_RUBRIC rows.** The L.7-6a…6m rows don't exist yet (the plan-phase rubric commit is pending). The rubricRows field above proposes all 13, grouped so every L-audiobook §5 acceptance falls under one of them.
